    });

    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();

<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

  function schedule(){

    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root){
    new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  setTimeout(run,120);
  setTimeout(run,650);

<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.

  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

    if(!nodes.length) return null;

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      fitText(el,17);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.r61-center').forEach(function(center){
      var value=ensureCenterValue(center);
      if(value) fitText(value,8.5);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root){
    new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  setTimeout(run,120);
  setTimeout(run,650);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R67_KPI_INLINE_UNIT_FULL_VALUE_JS">
(function(){
  function wrapKpiValue(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var existing=el.querySelector('.ref-kpi-value');
    if(existing) return existing;

    var small=el.querySelector('small');
    var nodes=[].slice.call(el.childNodes).filter(function(n){
      return n.nodeType===3 && String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();
    });
    if(!nodes.length) return null;

    var value=nodes.map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim()}).join(' ').trim();
    if(!value) return null;

    nodes.forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.className='ref-kpi-value';
    span.textContent=value;
    if(small) el.insertBefore(span,small); else el.appendChild(span);
    return span;
  }

  function fitValue(el,span){
    if(!el || !span || !span.isConnected) return;

    var unit=el.querySelector('small');
    span.style.removeProperty('font-size');

    var base=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(span).fontSize)||28;
    var minSize=13.5;
    var boxWidth=Math.floor(el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
    var unitWidth=unit?Math.ceil(unit.getBoundingClientRect().width):0;
    var gap=unit?4:0;
    var available=Math.max(1,boxWidth-unitWidth-gap);

    span.style.fontSize=base+'px';
    var natural=span.scrollWidth;

    if(natural>available){
      var size=Math.max(minSize,Math.min(base,Math.floor((base*available/natural)*10)/10));
      span.style.fontSize=size+'px';

      var guard=0;
      while(span.scrollWidth>available+0.5 && size>minSize && guard<60){
        size=Math.max(minSize,size-0.25);
        span.style.fontSize=size+'px';
        guard++;
      }
    }
  }

  function run(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi .ref-spark').forEach(function(el){
      el.remove();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.ref-kpi-number').forEach(function(el){
      var span=wrapKpiValue(el);
      if(span) fitValue(el,span);
    });
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      run();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

  var root=document.getElementById('root');
  if(root) new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});

  setTimeout(run,60);
  setTimeout(run,250);
  setTimeout(run,800);
})();
</script>
<script id="AUREL_R78_REAL_SLIDER_JS">
(function(){
  var HERO=[
    'assets/aurel_hero_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_hero_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/1c6c2359-51ca-4c5d-82ac-2e7d75c5b931-aurel_hero_sunrise_webfit_20261001.png?v=R93-COVER-TOP-20261001-1150',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/f79a6502-e2f6-45c6-b246-55153447494c-aurel_t4_user_exact_t3frame_2172x724.webp?v=R101-USER-T4-EXACT-T3-FRAME',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/3a3f1d3e-d848-4d8b-9d49-7f11ddb871f1-aurel_t5_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/d8f2c217-e524-4c8a-9c7b-8199370353bd-aurel_t6_hanoi_hero_user_exact_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b16bf185-0e02-4922-a09d-e7fed0b72dcc-aurel_t7_danang_hero_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8a8c15cb-73f1-4699-b2c3-9d604be3f29d-aurel_t8_danang_hero_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/845429f8-f5df-40ca-ba6e-becfe004a7e7-aurel_t5_hanoi_sidebar_morning_20261001.png?v=R102-T5-HANOI-MORNING',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/377dcc56-bd1c-4ada-92f6-91900b46756f-aurel_t6_hanoi_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R106-T6-HANOI-NIGHT',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/409534f9-e53a-4146-8486-1efcb0fbb763-aurel_t7_danang_sidebar_sunrise_20261001.png?v=R108-T7-DANANG-SUNRISE',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
